Abstract: AI research agents (AIRA) can now propose, implement, and evaluate their own machine learning experiments, but progress on frontier tasks is throttled by cost: a candidate solution can be written in minutes, whereas evaluating it can take hours to days of GPU time. An agent can therefore propose far more candidates than it can afford to run, and its progress depends on its research preference: how it allocates a fixed execution budget across many candidates. We introduce AI Research Preference Models (RPMs) that predict which of multiple candidate solutions are most worth executing, without paying the cost of executing them all. We build RPMs from frozen pretrained language models (with no task-specific training), in two forms: an inference-only model that reasons over candidate plans, code, and prior executed solutions, and an agentic model that additionally runs small-scale pilot experiments before deciding. We integrate both into the AIRA-dojo search agent and evaluate on AIRS-Bench, a recent benchmark of machine learning research tasks for AI research agents. The two variants raise the average normalized score from 0.684 to 0.711 and 0.729 respectively, and reach the unguided agent's 24-hour performance in roughly 15 hours, using less than two-thirds of its execution budget. Our best RPMs also yield new state-of-the-art results on two AIRS-Bench tasks.

</details>


### [137] [HELIX: Model-Harness Co-evolution for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13951)
*Tianyu Fan,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HELIX，一个可溯源、支持演化的智能体运行框架（harness）。HELIX 将智能体系统分解为类型化端口、可复用原子、配方、产品外壳和运行时策略，使得干预显式可审计，同时保留轨迹、测试结果和来源信息。作者在代码修复任务上进行了一轮演化实验：65 个候选的搜索组合发现了相对 Pi 提升 4.0% 任务覆盖率的基础 harness，而整个组合通过互补兄弟行为额外暴露高达 58.0% 的已验证覆盖率；200 槽位兄弟切片产生 438 条已验证的 SFT、critic、filter 和偏好记录。结果表明 harness、模型与数据构成反馈系统：harness 演化扩展当前能力并为下一代模型提供学习信号，模型更新又推动下一轮 harness 演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规模化智能体能力的研究大多聚焦于改进模型本身，但交互式智能体实际运行在运行时 harness 之上——harness 决定了上下文、工具、控制流和停止条件，既影响模型能完成什么，也决定模型从哪些轨迹中学习。这种耦合关系促使模型与 harness 需协同演化以实现递归自我改进，但缺少一种可控的方式来演化 harness，同时保持干预的身份标识与效果的可追溯性。

Method: HELIX 将智能体系统抽象为五类要素：类型化端口（typed ports）、可复用原子（atoms）、配方（recipes）、产品外壳（product shells）和运行时策略（runtime policies）。这些分解使得改动（interventions）显式化且可审计，并保留轨迹、测试结果和来源信息（provenance）。Harness 演化被设计为双重角色：一方面改进固定模型下的执行能力，另一方面产生成功的匹配、回归、近似失败（near misses）和替代解，作为后续模型改进的训练数据。实验中，通过 65 个候选的组合搜索选出基础 harness，并用 200 槽位兄弟切片收集 SFT、critic、filter 和偏好记录。

Result: 在代码修复任务上，65 个候选组合发现的基础 harness 相比 Pi 将任务覆盖率提升 4.0%；完整组合通过互补的兄弟行为额外暴露了最多 58.0% 的已验证覆盖率。200 槽位的兄弟切片产出 438 条已验证的 SFT、critic、filter 和偏好训练记录。所选候选通过重复运行和 SWE-bench 评估器验证。

Conclusion: HELIX 证明了 harness、模型和数据三者构成反馈闭环：harness 演化在当下扩展智能体能力，同时产生下一轮模型训练所需的学习信号；模型更新反过来激励新一轮 harness 演化。HELIX 为该递归过程提供了可审计的接口，是研究模型-harness 协同演化的基础平台。代码开源在 https://github.com/HKUDS/HELIX。

Abstract: Scaling agent capability has largely focused on improving the model, yet an interactive agent acts through a runtime harness that mediates context, tools, control flow, and stopping. The harness shapes both what a model can accomplish and the trajectories from which it learns. This coupling motivates model-harness co-evolution for recursive self-improvement: build harnesses for a fixed model, update the model from verified sibling trajectories, and rebuild the harnesses as model capabilities change. Realizing this loop requires a controlled way to evolve harnesses while preserving intervention identity and effect. We present HELIX, a source-traceable substrate for harness evolution. HELIX decomposes agent systems into typed ports, reusable atoms, recipes, product shells, and runtime policies. It makes interventions explicit and auditable while retaining trajectories, test outcomes, and provenance. Harness evolution thus serves two linked roles: improving fixed-model execution and producing matched successes, regressions, near misses, and alternative solutions as data for subsequent model improvement. We evaluate HELIX in one evolution round on code repair. A 65-candidate portfolio discovers a fixed harness that improves task coverage by 4.0% over Pi, while the full portfolio exposes up to 58.0% more verified coverage through complementary sibling behavior. Selected candidates are assessed with repeated runs and the SWE-bench evaluator. A 200-slot sibling slice yields 438 verified SFT, critic, filter, and preference records. These results show how harness, model, and data form a feedback system: harness evolution expands current capability and creates learning signal for the next model; model updates motivate the next round of harness evolution. HELIX provides an auditable interface for studying this recursive process. Code is available at https://github.com/HKUDS/HELIX.

</details>


### [138] [Implementing Computational Law in Wolfram Language for the Governance of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13958)
*James K. Wiles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何治理推理过程无法完全检查的AI系统，提出治理不要求理解系统推理，而需明确系统义务、许可与禁止行为并检查其合规性。作者用Wolfram语言实现了Reified Input/Output Logic，并测试GPT-4将英文法律陈述翻译为该形式体系的能力，记录了各种失败模式，最后通过"AI看门狗"案例展示形式化规则如何从合同延伸到智能体操作代码，产生可审计的符号化行为理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统的推理过程往往不透明（黑盒），传统依赖完全理解系统内部推理来实施治理的路径不可行。作者提出应转向一种不依赖推理可解释性、而是基于明确规范与合规检查的治理范式，即通过形式化表达系统的义务、许可与禁止行为来实施治理。

Method: 1）在Wolfram Language中实现了Reified Input/Output Logic（DAPRECO知识库背后的形式体系），涵盖核心I/O公理、义务、许可、构成性规范、reified事件及时间算子；2）测试GPT-4将英文法律陈述翻译为该形式体系的能力，并归类失败模式；3）通过一个在计算合同下运行的"AI看门狗"智能体进行案例研究，展示规则如何从合同直接扩展到运行时代码。

Result: GPT-4的翻译存在四类失败：幻觉函数、遗漏时间范围、偏离形式体系，以及最严重的情况下——代码能运行、读起来合理但静默编码了错误规范。案例研究表明形式化规则可以从合同直接延伸到具身智能体的操作代码中，产生符号化可审计的行为理由。

Conclusion: 作者论证计算法学可以作为AI治理工具，并提出理想目标是实现"应当且能够以程序化方式执行的法律"的形式化。

Abstract: How do we govern AI systems whose reasoning we cannot fully inspect? Governance does not require understanding a system's reasoning. It requires stating what the system is obliged, permitted, and forbidden to do, and checking whether it complied. I present an implementation of Reified Input/Output Logic, the formalism behind the DAPRECO knowledge base, in Wolfram Language: the core I/O axioms, obligations, permissions, constitutive norms, reified eventualities, and temporal operators. I then test whether GPT-4 can translate English legal statements into the formalism, and report the failures: hallucinated functions, omitted temporal scope, deviation from the formalism, and (in the worst cases) code that runs, reads plausibly, but silently encodes the wrong norm. A case study, an AI guard dog operating under a computational contract, shows how formalized rules can extend from a contract directly into the operational code of an embodied agent, producing symbolic, auditable justifications for its behaviour. I argue that computational law can be used as a governance tool and that a desirable goal would be to formalize the law that can and ought to be programmatically executable.

</details>


### [139] [Nanbeige4.2-3B on Apple Silicon: Fixing Deployment Bugs and Decreasing Looped Transformer Memory Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13987)
*John T. Halloran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析并修复了Nanbeige4.2-3B（基于循环Transformer的3B参数智能体模型）在Apple Silicon（MPS）上运行时的五个独立bug，并提出分块预填充策略将可用上下文宽度扩展2.7倍，使模型在MCPMark上完成率从0%提升至30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nanbeige4.2-3B作为一个参数高效的智能体模型，其发布的checkpoint在Hugging Face transformers框架下无法直接运行，存在多个bug。此外，其循环Transformer的层复用策略虽实现参数效率，但导致峰值注意力内存翻倍，限制了上下文长度，影响智能体任务的实际可用性。

Method: 作者在Apple Silicon (MPS)上对模型进行系统化调试，识别并修复了五个独立bug（包括RoPE缓冲区被静默置零和调用已移除的transformers缓存API等问题）。针对层复用导致的内存瓶颈，提出分块预填充（chunked-prefill）策略来降低峰值内存占用，使上下文宽度在32 GiB共享内存上扩展2.7倍。还修复了系统提示词和MPS原生内存问题，并发布了补丁后的checkpoint、系统提示词优化器和评测工具。

Result: 修复后的模型在MCPMark子集上完成了最高30%的真实智能体任务（原始模型为0%），在BFCL基准上单工具调用接近完美但多数多工具测试失败。分块预填充策略将可用上下文宽度扩展2.7倍。

Conclusion: 即使修复了所有bug，仅靠补丁不足以让模型胜任智能体任务，根本问题在于循环Transformer层复用策略带来的内存开销。分块预填充可缓解此问题，但模型在多工具智能体场景下仍存在明显不足，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Nanbeige4.2-3B is a 3B-parameter agentic model built around a Looped Transformer (LT) that reuses one stack of layers for a second forward pass, adding effective depth without additional parameters. Evaluated on Apple Silicon (MPS), we identify five independent bugs which prevent the released checkpoint from running via Hugging Face transformers out of the box (including a silently-zeroed RoPE buffer and calls to removed transformers cache APIs). Furthermore, we show that fixing these bugs is still not sufficient for agentic tasks, due to the LT's layer-reuse strategy (which effectively doubles peak attention memory) used to achieve parameter efficiency. We thus introduce a chunked-prefill strategy which alleviates the incurred memory-capacity penalty, extending allowable context width by $2.7 \times$ on 32~GiB shared memory. However, even with the reduced memory overhead, we show that patches are required to render Nanbeige4.2-3B usable; resolving both system prompt and MPS-native memory bugs finally allows reliable evaluation on standard MCP and tool-calling benchmarks. On a subset of MCPMark, the debugged model completes up to 30\% of real agentic tasks (up from the original's 0\%), while, on BFCL, it is near-perfect at single tool calls (yet fails the majority of multi-tool tests). We release the patched checkpoint, system prompt optimizer, and evaluation harnesses at https://github.com/johnhalloran321/Nanbeige4.2-3B-mps-fix.

</details>


### [140] [Content Depth Matters in Short-Video Recommendation: Rethinking the Attention Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13990)
*Liwei Deng,Jing Jiang,Zhiwei Li,Yang Wang,Guodong Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出内容深度评分（CDS）指标来量化短视频的认知深度，并构建了首个短视频推荐内容深度评估基准 SCOPE-Bench，包含15万条带CDS标注的视频。通过评估13个代表性推荐系统，发现现有算法普遍偏好浅层内容，且对深度内容的推荐能力仅略优于随机选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频推荐系统为了最大化用户参与度而优化，倾向于推荐能迅速吸引注意力的浅层内容，但长期暴露于这类内容可能损害用户的认知参与和心理健康。现有推荐系统缺乏从认知内容角度评估的指标和基准，无法量化其对用户认知深度的影响。

Method: 提出CDS指标，基于认知心理学和学习理论建立七级量表来度量短视频刺激高阶认知过程的程度；构建SCOPE-Bench基准，基于大规模开源短视频数据集为15万条视频提供CDS标注，用于系统评估推荐系统的认知内容表现。

Result: 基于SCOPE-Bench评估了13个代表性推荐系统，发现它们一致偏好浅层内容；推荐认知深度内容的表现仅略优于随机选择，揭示了现有推荐目标中此前被忽视的局限性。

Conclusion: 现有短视频推荐系统在内容深度方面存在明显不足，CDS和SCOPE-Bench为从认知视角评估和改进推荐系统提供了新的基准与工具，有助于推动推荐系统向促进深度认知参与的方向发展。

Abstract: Driven by the attention economy, short-video Recommender Systems (RSs) are primarily optimized to maximize user engagement by promoting videos that capture attention within seconds. These systems inherently favor shallow-content videos that are effective at attracting immediate attention. However, growing evidence suggests that prolonged exposure to such content may negatively affect users' cognitive engagement and mental well-being, raising concerns about the long-term societal impact of the short-video platform. To tackle this challenge, this paper introduces a new metric, the \textbf{Content Depth Score (CDS)}, to quantify the content depth of short videos. CDS measures the extent to which a video is expected to stimulate higher-order cognitive processes, using a seven-level scale grounded in established theories of cognitive psychology and learning. As an initial step toward this vision, we present \textbf{SCOPE-Bench}, the first benchmark for content-depth evaluation in short-video recommendation. Built upon a large-scale open-source short-video dataset, SCOPE-Bench provides CDS annotations for 150K videos, enabling systematic evaluation of RSs from a cognitive-content perspective. Leveraging SCOPE-Bench, we evaluate 13 representative RSs and reveal a consistent preference for shallow-content videos. Moreover, we find that these algorithms recommending cognitively deep content are only marginally better than random selection, highlighting a previously overlooked limitation of existing recommendation objectives. Our code and datasets are available at https://liweidengdavid.github.io/SCOPE-Bench/.

</details>


### [141] [Simulation-Driven Vehicular Traffic Data Augmentation: Extending Sensor Coverage Through Virtual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13993)
*Davide Andrea Guastella,Eladio Montero Porras,Evangelos Pournaras,Gianluca Bontempi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于仿真的数据增强方法，在城市路网中为每个物理传感器生成虚拟替身传感器，扩增交通流量数据集，解决传感器空间覆盖率不足导致的机器学习泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通管理依赖传感器网络，但传感器空间覆盖受部署成本和隐私法规限制，稀疏数据训练的模型无法泛化到未监测区域，且传感器基础设施变化时需重新训练。

Method: 为每个物理传感器在路网中寻找替代位置放置虚拟传感器，使用图搜索启发式算法，联合优化车流连续性和原位置与替代位置之间的交通指标相似度，同时强制最小空间位移以保障观测交通条件的多样性。在布鲁塞尔（校准模型）和那慕尔（合成模型）两座比利时城市上验证。

Result: 增强后的数据集保留了观测位置的每日双峰需求分布及交通动态特性。

Conclusion: 基于仿真的虚拟传感器数据增强方法能有效扩展传感器空间覆盖，在不改变物理基础设施的前提下生成多样化的交通计数数据，有助于提升机器学习模型在未监测位置的泛化能力。

Abstract: Urban traffic management relies on sensor networks whose spatial coverage is limited by deployment costs and privacy regulations. Machine learning models trained on such sparse data cannot generalize to unmonitored locations and must be retrained whenever the sensor infrastructure changes. We propose a simulation-based methodology that addresses this problem by generating augmented traffic count datasets in which each physical sensor is replaced by a virtual sensor placed at a surrogate location in the road network. Virtual sensors are selected by a graph-search heuristic that jointly maximises vehicle-flow continuity and traffic-metric similarity between the original and surrogate locations, while enforcing a minimum spatial displacement to ensure diversity of observed traffic conditions. We validate the method on two Belgian cities: Brussels, using a calibrated model, and Namur, using synthetic models. The augmented datasets preserve the bimodal daily demand profile and the dynamics of traffic at the observed locations.

</details>


### [142] [Buy the Rumor, Sell the News: When Is News Priced In?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14014)
*Alireza Kargarzadeh,Nariman Khaledian,Navid Parvini,Sid Ghatak,Arman Khaledian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用大语言模型对457万条金融新闻进行分类标注，在覆盖3000只美股、168万条股票-日事件的数据集上验证了两个传统市场谚语——"新闻发布时价格已消化完毕"和"谣言买入、新闻卖出"。研究发现：价格变动集中在新闻发布前和发布当天；市场对量化基本面新闻（盈余、股息等）反应不足、持续漂移数周，而对软性故事类新闻（产品发布、宏观评论等）反应过度、随后回吐；新闻在发布前抬升波动率、发布后降低波动率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证市场谚语"新闻发布时价格已消化"和"谣言买入、新闻卖出"是否成立、适用于哪些类型的新闻以及影响程度多大，回答市场吸收公开信息速度快慢的基本问题。

Method: 使用大语言模型teacher通过主动学习蒸馏出紧凑分类器，将每条新闻标注为17种事件标签和5种属性；将文章聚类为故事以区分首次报道与后续跟踪报道；测量beta调整后的异常收益，包含364,405条中性情绪事件作为安慰剂对照组；覆盖457万条新闻、3000只美股（2023-2026年）、168万条股票-日事件。

Result: 三方面结果：(1) 新闻相关的价格变动集中在发布前和发布当天，发布日累计变动是20天后的2.8倍；谣言标记事件中谣言日捕获全部变动而随后的确认无贡献。(2) 与安慰剂对照相比，市场对数字（量化）过度反应不足——基本面新闻（盈余、股息、指引、分析师动作）持续沿新闻方向漂移数周；对故事反应过度——软性新闻（发布、宏观评论、领导层变动）随后回吐涨幅。(3) 新闻具有宽度和方向双重效应：发布前抬升波动率，发布后波动率下降，因为发布解决了不确定性。此外产出每个事件标签的漂移量表，可作为新闻条件预测模型的先验。

Conclusion: 实证支持两个市场谚语的成立，揭示了市场在新闻发布前已定价、量化消息与软性故事定价机制的系统性差异，并展示了LLM在金融文本分析中的规模化应用价值。

Abstract: Two old market sayings hold that news is already priced in by the time it is published, and that the rumor is bought while the news is sold. Both place the price move associated with a piece of news before and at publication rather than after it. Whether the claims hold, for which kinds of news, and by how much are basic questions about how fast markets absorb public information. We test them on 4.57 million financial news articles covering roughly 3,000 US stocks (2023-2026). A large language model teacher, distilled into a compact classifier through active learning, assigns each article one of 17 event tags and five attributes; articles are clustered into stories to separate first reports from follow-up coverage; and beta-adjusted abnormal returns are measured around the resulting 1.68 million stock-day events, with 364,405 neutral-sentiment events as a placebo group. Three results follow. First, the price move associated with news concentrates before and at publication: pooled across all signed events, the cumulative move in the news direction by the close of publication day is 2.8 times its value 20 days later, and for rumor-flagged events the rumor day captures the entire move while the subsequent confirmation contributes nothing. Second, measured against the placebo of comparable stocks, markets underreact to numbers and overreact to stories: quantified fundamental news (earnings, dividends, guidance, analyst actions) keeps drifting in the direction of the news for weeks, while soft story-driven news (launches, macro commentary, leadership) gives back its move. Third, news carries width as well as direction: publicity raises volatility before the publication day, and volatility declines once the news is out, because publication resolves uncertainty. The study also produces a table of measured drift for each event tag, usable as a prior in news-conditioned forecasting models.

</details>


### [143] [Regime-Conditional Verification: Correctness Estimation for Adapting and Monitoring Safety Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14089)
*Thiago Sandoval,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RCV是一种轻量级包装器方法，通过估计安全分类器预测与部署者策略不一致的概率，自适应地修正错误预测，同时利用相同的正确性估计信号检测分布漂移并触发维护循环，无需重训练基座分类器即可提升策略遵从度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的大语言模型安全分类器常因两方面的原因失效：一是分类器的决策反映的是训练时学习的策略而非部署者期望的策略，二是随着部署流量的演化分类器性能会退化。现有方法缺乏在不重训练分类器的前提下自适应解决这两类问题的方案。

Method: RCV从分类器内部表征中估计每个预测与部署者策略不一致的概率，并选择性地修正可能错误的预测。相同的正确性估计还作为无标签信号用于检测分布漂移，构成维护循环：更新正确性估计层，仅在必要时才进行分类器微调。

Result: 在三个现成安全分类器和两个基准数据集上，RCV在每种分类器-数据集组合中均提升了部署者策略遵从度，可捕获最多0.81的先前遗漏的不安全内容且无需修改底层分类器。在包含十个攻击活动的部署研究中，RCV在注入面板中检测到每个活动；在维护普查中，大多数漂移事件无需更新分类器即可修复，微调仅保留给修复无法恢复的残余事件。

Conclusion: RCV提供了一种无需重训练即可使现成安全分类器适配部署者策略并应对分布漂移的有效方案，通过轻量级的正确性估计和选择性修正，实现了更好的策略一致性、漂移检测和维护效率。

Abstract: Safety classifiers deployed with large language models often fail for two reasons: their decisions reflect the policy learned during training rather than the deployer's desired policy, and their performance degrades as deployment traffic evolves. We present Regime-Conditional Verification (RCV), a lightweight wrapper that adapts an off-the-shelf safety classifier without retraining it. RCV estimates, from the classifier's internal representations, the probability that each prediction disagrees with the deployer's policy, and selectively corrects predictions likely to be wrong. The same correctness estimates also provide a label-free signal for detecting distribution shift, enabling a maintenance loop that updates the correctness estimation layer and resorts to classifier fine-tuning only when necessary. Across three off-the-shelf safety classifiers and two benchmark datasets, RCV improves adherence to the deployer's policy in every classifier-dataset combination, catching up to 0.81 of previously missed unsafe content without modifying the underlying classifier. In a deployment study with ten attack campaigns, each a harm category held out of RCV's training, RCV detects every campaign in a dedicated injection panel; in the maintenance census most drift episodes are repaired without updating the classifier, and the fine-tune is reserved for the residual episodes that repair does not restore.

</details>


### [144] [Residual Dominance as a Structural Account of Last-Item Reliance in Causal Self-Attention Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14021)
*Keito Kozaki,Keigo Sakurai,Ren Togo,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文诊断了基于Transformer的序列推荐器（如SASRec）在推理时对最后交互项的过度依赖现象，通过范数分析和推理时残差缩放实验，揭示了"残差主导"（residual dominance）机制：残差加和使全块表示偏向同位置贡献，从而在结构上解释了极端的最后一项依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer序列推荐器在推理时严重依赖最近的交互项，但这一行为在预测所用的表示中如何结构化地表达仍然不清楚。作者希望通过结合推理时诊断和基于范数的全注意力块分析，提供一种结构化的解释。

Method: 采用三方面方法：(1) 推理时诊断，展示SASRec类模型的局部化最后一项依赖；(2) 对全注意力块进行范数分析，发现残差加和使表示强烈偏向同位置贡献（即残差主导）；(3) 以推理时残差缩放作为受控的诊断干预，观察结构性混合与最后项依赖之间的单调权衡关系。

Result: 改变残差强度会诱发结构混合与最后项依赖之间的单调权衡；降低残差强度可恢复一部分最终位置预测失败的案例（在这些案例中非最终位置的表示已经能正确排序真实物品）。

Conclusion: 提供了将极端最后项依赖与推理时残差主导联系起来的结构化解释，揭示了残差连接在该类模型中承担的关键角色。

Abstract: Transformer-based sequential recommenders with causal self-attention often rely heavily on the most recent interaction at inference time, but how this behavior is structurally expressed in the representation used for prediction remains unclear. We combine prediction-time diagnostics with norm-based analysis of the full attention block. First, we show that SASRec-style models exhibit highly localized last-item reliance. We then find that, although self-attention aggregates contextual information, residual addition sharply shifts the full-block representation toward same-position contributions, which we term residual dominance. To probe this interpretation, we use inference-time residual scaling as a controlled diagnostic intervention. Changing the residual strength induces a monotonic trade-off between structural mixing and last-item reliance, while reducing residual strength recovers a subset of final-position misses for which representations at non-final positions already rank the ground-truth item correctly. Our results provide a structural account linking extreme last-item reliance to residual dominance at inference time. The code is publicly available.

</details>


### [145] [MathForm: Scaling Mathematical Autoformalization with Knowledge Retrieval and Verification-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14221)
*Lushi Pu,Weiming Zhang,Xinheng Xie,Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Hengyu Zhao,Hongya Lyu,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Yudong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MathForm框架，通过Mathlib知识检索和验证引导的迭代精炼来构建经过验证的自动形式化训练数据，并构建了包含约367K条Lean 4验证样例的FormalVerse数据集。训练的MathForm-8B模型在六个基准上平均Pass@8分别达到88.06%（语法检查）和72.37%（一致性检查），超越多个专门的32B自动形式化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化方法过度依赖模型参数记忆中的库特定知识，且常见的数据构建管线缺乏反馈驱动的修订机制，导致难以进行忠实的数学语句形式化，即无法正确映射数学概念到Mathlib中复杂的类型和定义层级，同时保证生成语句保留源命题的语义。

Method: MathForm框架包含两部分：(1) 生成前检索规划器从Mathlib检索相关定义和现有形式化来指导形式化生成器；(2) 生成后使用编译器诊断和语义一致性反馈对生成语句进行迭代修订。基于此框架构建含约367K条验证样例的FormalVerse数据集，并通过监督微调加强化学习训练MathForm-8B模型。

Result: MathForm-8B在六个基准上平均Pass@8达88.06%（语法检查SC）和72.37%（一致性检查CC），超越多个专门的32B自动形式化模型。在具有挑战性的FATE-H和FATE-X子集上，CC通过率分别为63%和37%，两项均超过最强的专门基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过知识检索引导生成和验证引导的迭代修订，可以显著提升自动形式化的数据质量和模型能力，即使参数量更小（8B）也能在自动形式化任务上超越更大规模的专门模型（32B）。

Abstract: Autoformalization is commonly framed as translating natural-language mathematical statements into machine-verifiable formal languages such as Lean 4. However, faithful formalization requires more than translation. Models must map mathematical concepts to the complex hierarchy of types and definitions in formal libraries such as Mathlib, while ensuring that generated statements preserve the meaning of the source propositions. Existing approaches struggle because they rely heavily on the model's parametric memory for library-specific knowledge, while common data construction pipelines often resort to filtering single-pass outputs and lack mechanisms for feedback-driven revision. To address these challenges, we introduce MathForm, an autoformalization framework for constructing verified training data through Mathlib knowledge retrieval and verification-guided iterative refinement. Before generation, a retrieval planner gathers relevant definitions and existing formalizations from Mathlib to guide the formalization generator. Generated statements are then revised using compiler diagnostics and semantic-consistency feedback. Using this framework, we construct FormalVerse, a Lean 4 dataset containing approximately 367K verified examples across diverse mathematical domains and sources. We then train MathForm-8B through supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning. Across six benchmarks, MathForm-8B achieves average Pass@8 rates of 88.06% under Syntax Check (SC) and 72.37% under Consistency Check (CC), outperforming multiple specialized 32B autoformalizers. On the challenging FATE-H and FATE-X subsets, it attains CC pass rates of 63% and 37%, exceeding the strongest specialized baselines in both cases.

</details>


### [146] [Agent-Orchestration in Autonomous Chip Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14035)
*Linyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将芯片设计超智能建模为庞大的"AI组织"这一理念，探索大语言模型与工具使用智能体在芯片设计行业的应用潜力，核心问题在于厘清该高端产业真正需要怎样的AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型和工具使用智能体的快速发展激发人们探索智能体在芯片设计中的潜力，但核心疑问是该高度复杂的产业究竟需要什么样的AI形态。

Method: 提出将芯片设计超智能构想为一个巨大的AI组织（AI-organization）的理念框架。

Result: 本文为摘要，尚未给出具体实验结果，主要提出概念性的AI组织范式。

Conclusion: 芯片设计行业的AI不应仅是单一智能体，而应是一个组织化的AI生态系统，以匹配该产业复杂协作的特征。

Abstract: Recent developments in large language models (LLMs) and tool-using agents encourage people to explore the potential of using agents in chip design. The core question is what kind of AI we really need in such a sophisticated industry.
  To this end, we bring the idea of modeling a chip-design superintelligence as an enormous \textit{AI-organization}.

</details>


### [147] [Grounding Without Corrective Control: Truth-Tracking Profiles for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14252)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文区分了"可回答性"（answerability）与"纠正控制"（corrective control）两个概念，提出通过"路径档案"（route profiles）分析大型语言模型表征能否追踪真理，并论证了这些区分对预测模型在特定任务上的表现与选择干预手段具有实证价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年研究表明大型语言模型的表征具备某种内容指涉或参考功能，但"接地"（grounding）可以在不提供实时纠正途径的情况下保证内容或指涉。本文旨在探讨这一"缺口"的后果——即表征虽被接地但缺乏可纠正性时，其对真理追踪意味着什么，以及如何在任务层面加以分析。

Method: 本文提出一个分析框架：首先定义"可回答性"——当目标与任务特定的配置所产生、接受或撤回的输出能被差异所影响时，输出是可回答的；其次定义"纠正控制"——仅在活跃且充分独立的路径能够检测并修复新出现差异时才成立；第三引入"路径档案"记录哪些路径约束该配置及其相互关系，以此支持对真理追踪的分析。文本仅限模型作为压力测试案例，文本训练模型继承证词、连贯性与先前纠正的模式，以此区分衍生可回答性（继承约束）与活性可回答性（当前路径对新鲜差异提供的关系）。

Result: 论文指出：在目标任务需要独立信息性事实访问时，应出现"流畅失败"（fluent failures）。自一致性、检索、工具使用、代码执行、多模态输入和反馈等干预应具有选择性帮助效果。路径与任务之间的交互可用于检验该框架的区分度。该分解的实证负担在于预测未见过的路径-任务组合或改善干预选择而不需要概念性重构。表面改进与真理追踪改进可能分化。

Conclusion: 作者认为可回答性与纠正控制的区分是分析语言模型真理追踪能力的核心工具。路径档案提供了结构化的分析手段，能够解释为何表面性能提升不等于真理追踪改善，并指导在何种任务上何种干预手段有效。文本仅限模型是这些概念的压力测试场景，而纯文本配置则构成任务相对的限制性用例。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that some large language model representations have content or reference. Grounding can secure either without supplying live routes for correction. This paper asks what follows from that gap. An output is answerable when discrepancies can affect what a target- and task-specific arrangement produces, accepts, or withdraws. The arrangement has corrective control only when live, sufficiently independent routes can detect and repair fresh discrepancies. A route profile records which routes constrain the arrangement and how they are related. Those profiles support analysis of truth-tracking: patterned support for representational success.
  Language models are the pressure case; text-only arrangements provide a task-relative limiting case. Text-trained models inherit patterns of testimony, coherence, and prior correction. Where target-sensitive correction survives training, these can supply derivative answerability (inherited constraint); live answerability is the relation supplied by a current route for fresh discrepancies. Fluent failures should follow when a task requires independently informative access to the facts. Self-consistency, retrieval, tools, code execution, multimodal input, and feedback should help selectively. Route-by-task interactions test the distinctions. The decomposition's empirical burden is to predict held-out route--task combinations or improve intervention choice without conceptual refitting. Surface improvement and truth-tracking improvement can come apart.

</details>


### [148] [Demystifying Agent Skills: Why They Work-Until They Don't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14036)
*Zhiyuan Jiang,Fangrui Huang,Hanwen Xing,Xander Wu,Yipeng Gao,Rui Cao,Mengdi Wang,Shilong Liu,Yijiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了技能（Skills）在LLM智能体推理时增强中的适用条件、作用机制与失效场景，通过受控实验和配对轨迹分析揭示：技能的主要作用是通过程序化锚定（procedural anchoring）稳定执行轨迹（占65.7%），而非注入缺失知识（仅4.5%）；检索是独立瓶颈（池从5增至100时，实际使用精度从29.6%降至3.3%）；技能在脆弱假设、不兼容上下文或适应性不足时会失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能评估大多仅关注聚合任务成功率是否提升，忽略了更根本的问题：技能何时有效、为何有效、在何处失效。作者旨在超越简单的成功率比较，深入理解技能的作用机理，为可靠的自进化智能体提供指导。

Method: 采用对比研究方法，结合受控定量实验与配对轨迹分析。在多基准、多智能体框架和多LLM上隔离表征、结果标注、检索难度和跨框架鲁棒性的影响。规范化8,135条受控实验试验记录，从240条开放式编码记录中保留238条有效唯一标签，归纳为3个高层次类别和12种技能使用模式。

Result: 技能在噪声轨迹中起到程序化锚定作用以稳定执行；在匹配比较中，技能比工作流记忆（Workflow Memory）提升6.06分。程序化锚定占技能案例的65.7%，显式知识注入仅占4.5%。检索池从5增至100时，实际使用精度从29.6%降至3.3%。混淆干扰项损害离线识别但下游成功保持稳定；精确的ground-truth调用既非充分也非必要条件。

Conclusion: 技能的核心机制是稳定动作而非注入知识；检索是需要独立优化的重要瓶颈；评估应超越聚合成功率，关注技能失效的具体原因（脆弱假设、不兼容上下文、适应不足），以引导可靠的自进化智能体发展。

Abstract: Skills have emerged as a practical and effective approach for enhancing LLM agents at inference time through structured packages of knowledge. However, existing evaluations largely measure whether skills improve aggregated task success, leaving a more fundamental question underexplored: \emph{\textbf{When do skills help, why do they work, and where do they fail?}} Through controlled experiments across various benchmarks, agent harnesses and LLMs, we isolate the effects of representation, outcome annotation, retrieval difficulty, and cross-framework robustness of skills. To further answer this question, we design a contrastive study that combines controlled quantitative experiments with paired trajectory analysis. We normalize 8,135 trial records from controlled experiments and retain 238 valid unique labels from 240 open-coded records. We consolidate these observations into a taxonomy of three high-level categories and twelve skill-use modes: skills work when noisy trajectories become procedural anchors that stabilize execution. Skills improve over Workflow Memory by 6.06 points in matched comparisons. Procedural anchoring accounts for 65.7\% of skill cases, versus 4.5\% for explicit knowledge injection, showing that skills stabilize action rather than inject missing facts. Retrieval is a separate bottleneck: as pools grow from 5 to 100, actual-use precision falls from 29.6\% to 3.3\%. Confusable distractors impair offline identification, yet downstream success remains stable; exact ground-truth invocation is neither sufficient nor necessary. Skills fail under brittle assumptions, incompatible contexts, or insufficient adaptation. These findings move evaluation beyond aggregate success rates and guide reliable self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [149] [Benchmarking data-driven material models on the classic Treloar dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14063)
*Hagen Holthusen,Moritz Flaschel,Denisa Martonová,Ellen Kuhl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用Treloar经典实验数据对六种基于机器学习的超弹性本构模型方法进行了系统基准测试，比较了拟合性能、计算成本、超参数敏感性和实现难度，并量化了预测精度与模型复杂度之间的权衡。结果表明所有方法均能很好地复现基准数据，但各有优劣，作者未选出单一最优方法，而是提供了实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习正在重塑本构建模领域，涌现出大量基于机器学习的超弹性材料建模方法，但缺乏系统性的公平比较。面对众多可选方案，研究者难以判断不同方法在实际应用中的相对表现、计算代价和适用场景，因此需要一个统一基准来评估这些方法。

Method: 以Treloar的经典橡胶超弹性实验数据为基准，对六种代表性机器学习方法进行系统比较：(1) 广义不变量本构人工神经网络（G-ICANN）、(2) 物理增强神经网络（PANN）、(3) 材料指纹（MF）、(4) 自适应材料指纹（AMF）、(5) 高效无监督本构律识别与发现（EULCLID）等。从拟合性能、计算成本、超参数敏感性、实现难度四个维度进行评估，并通过材料参数数量和模型评估时间两方面量化模型复杂度。

Result: 所有六种方法均能出色地复现Treloar的基准实验数据，在预测精度上差异不大。然而各方法在计算成本、超参数敏感性和实现难度方面存在显著差异。研究发现预测精度与模型复杂度之间存在明显权衡：精度更高的模型往往需要更多材料参数或更长的计算时间。

Conclusion: 不存在单一的最优方法，各方法各有其优势和局限性。作者根据比较结果提供了针对不同实际应用场景的方法选择指导，并公开了全部源代码、训练脚本、比较脚本、结果和数据，以促进可复现性和进一步研究。

Abstract: Machine learning is rapidly reshaping constitutive modeling, offers new ways to learn material behavior directly from experimental data, and challenges long-established modeling paradigms. But with a growing number of machine-learning-based approaches available, how do they compare in practice? In this paper, we use the classic experimental data of Treloar to benchmark popular frameworks for hyperelasticity: (Generalized-Invariant) Constitutive Artificial Neural Networks, Physics-Augmented Neural Networks, (Adaptive) Material Fingerprinting, and Efficient Unsupervised Constitutive Law Identification & Discovery. We compare their fitting performance, computational cost, hyperparameter sensitivity, and ease of implementation. Furthermore, we discuss the trade-offs between predictive accuracy and model complexity. The latter is assessed by quantifying both the number of material parameters in the discovered models and the computational time required to evaluate the constitutive model and its derivatives. The results show that all methods can reproduce the benchmark data remarkably well. Rather than identifying a single winner, we highlight the strengths and limitations of each approach and provide practical guidance for their use. The source code for all six methods, including the training and comparison scripts, as well as all results and data used in this study, is publicly available via https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21915635.

</details>


### [150] [AnchorBench: A Multi-Pathway Benchmark for the Anchoring Effect in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14320)
*Yiderigun Borjigin,Alexander Hermann,Christian Cyron,Roland Aydin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AnchorBench基准测试，系统地评估大语言模型中的锚定效应，在显式的锚定相关性轴下测试多种锚定路径，覆盖14个模型（10个开源+4个前沿API模型），发现锚定效应具有强路径依赖性等关键规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM锚定效应的研究通常只评估少量的锚定路径，且很少区分无关锚定与合理（plausible）锚定，缺乏系统性的评测框架。

Method: 构建AnchorBench基准，在显式的锚定相关性轴上评估多种锚定路径；使用大量受控提示词，对14个模型（10个开源权重模型和4个前沿API模型）进行系统评测。

Result: (1)锚定效应强烈依赖锚定路径；(2)通过较强路径引入时，合理锚定通常比无关锚定产生更大的偏移；(3)锚定影响随锚定值偏离证据支持的答案越远而减弱，在External和RAG路径上最明显；(4)高任务准确率（控制条件下Acc₁₀>95%）并不能保证对合理锚定的鲁棒性，即使是前沿API模型也仍然易受影响。

Conclusion: 锚定效应在LLM中是普遍且路径依赖的现象，合理锚定对模型鲁棒性构成持久威胁，且与任务准确率正交，需要专门针对锚定鲁棒性的改进。

Abstract: The anchoring effect is a cognitive bias in which an initial reference value shifts a later judgment toward itself. This effect is well established in human judgment and decision-making, and recent work suggests that large language models (LLMs) exhibit similar behavior. However, existing work on anchoring in LLMs typically evaluates only a narrow set of anchor pathways and rarely distinguishes irrelevant from plausible anchors. We introduce AnchorBench, a benchmark for the anchoring effect in LLMs that evaluates multiple anchor pathways under an explicit anchor relevance axis. Across fourteen models, including ten open-weight models and four frontier API models, and a large set of controlled prompts, we find that (1) anchoring is strongly pathway-dependent, (2) plausible anchors usually induce larger shifts than irrelevant ones when introduced through stronger pathways, (3) anchor influence generally weakens as the anchor moves farther from the evidence-supported answer, most clearly on External and RAG, and (4) high task accuracy on the anchor-free control condition (Acc$_{10}$: answers within 10 points of gold) does not guarantee robustness: even frontier API models above 95% control accuracy remain susceptible to plausible anchors.

</details>


### [151] [Scaling Domain Data Repetition in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14071)
*Jingwei Li,Xinran Gu,Rui Dai,Xintong Hao,Chengyin Xu,Yan Wu,Shuran Zheng,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了LLM扩展中高质量领域数据的重复训练策略。发现固定TPP下，最优重复次数随模型规模轻微增加；领域的最优重复次数与最终验证损失强负相关，而与唯一数据量弱相关。建议可在小规模代理模型上以相同TPP调优重复次数来估算大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM规模扩大，训练token预算需按比例增长以维持合适的tokens-per-parameter（TPP）比率。然而高质量领域数据难以像通用网页数据那样规模扩展，导致其在训练混合中的占比下降。重复使用已有高质量数据是抵消稀释的有效手段，但过度重复可能导致过拟合，因此需要系统研究重复次数与模型规模、领域特性之间的最优权衡。

Method: 在训练token预算随模型规模成比例增长的实际LLM扩展场景下，对固定领域和跨领域进行了系统实验。固定领域实验中考察TPP不变时最优重复次数随模型规模的变华；跨领域实验中分析最优重复次数与最终验证损失和唯一数据量之间的相关性。

Result: 1) 固定TPP下，最优重复次数随模型规模增大而轻微增加，这一发现出乎意料；2) 跨领域中，最优重复次数与最终验证损失呈强负相关——损失越低的领域可受益于更多的重复；3) 唯一领域数据量与最优重复次数仅弱相关。

Conclusion: 最优重复次数可主要依据领域验证损失来估计，且在小规模代理模型上以相同TPP调优重复次数可为大规模模型提供实用的估算参考，为大规模预训练中的数据处理策略提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: As large language models scale, their training-token budgets must also increase to maintain an appropriate tokens-per-parameter ratio (\(\mathrm{TPP}\)). However, high-quality domain data is much harder to scale than general web data. As model size and the training-token budget increase, its fraction in the training mixture tends to decrease. Repeating the available high-quality data provides an effective way to counteract this dilution, but excessive repetition may lead to overfitting. We study this trade-off under practical LLM scaling, where the training-token budget grows proportionally with model size. For a fixed domain, we first find that, surprisingly at a fixed \(\mathrm{TPP}\), the optimal repetition count mildly increases with model size. Across different domains, we find that the optimal repetition count is strongly negatively correlated with the final validation loss of a domain: domains with lower loss can generally benefit from more repetitions. In contrast, the amount of unique domain data is only weakly related to the optimal repetition count. These findings suggest that repetition counts tuned on smaller proxy models with the same \(\mathrm{TPP}\) can provide a practical estimate for larger models.

</details>


### [152] [Wrong but Useful: Trajectory Value Beyond Answer Correctness in Multi-Agent Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14375)
*Chih-Hsuan Yang,Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Cheng-Hau Yang,Weijian Zheng,Fernando Llorente,Xiaolong Ma,Xinyang Li,Eliu A. Huerta,Ian T. Foster,Rajeev Thakur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DHD（Diverse Hypothesis Deliberation）协议，用于测量多智能体推理系统中每个消息的"轨迹价值"——即消息可用与否对下游推理的影响，而非仅基于正确性过滤。研究发现错误的但有用的消息普遍存在，超过四成的正确性改变是正面的，且此现象可重复（p=0.0002）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体推理系统通过一致性、置信度或自动评分来过滤消息，但这一做法隐含假设"正确的消息就是值得保留的"。然而，错误答案可能包含有用的分解、约束或科学原理。作者希望区分消息的"正确性"与"轨迹价值"（对后续推理的影响），弥补现有评估指标的缺失。

Method: 提出DHD（Diverse Hypothesis Deliberation）控制测量协议：缓存五个独立生成的消息，通过重放同一下游求解器（integrator），分别在每个消息可用或隐藏的条件下进行比较，从而测量每个消息的轨迹价值。在5个数学和科学基准、2个开放模型家族（gpt-oss-120b和gemma-4-31B-it）上进行实验，并通过受控重复实验验证结果的稳健性。

Result: 在每一个基准-模型组合中均出现"错误但有帮助"的消息。在改变最终正确性的错误答案消息中，每个模型中超过四成的改变是有帮助的。受控重复表明可重复的消息效应不太可能仅由重放变化引起（p=0.0002）。针对可重复的"错误但有帮助"消息的干预实验发现，完整消息效果最佳，保留推理过程比仅保留答案能保留更多成功。轨迹价值证据能比单纯答案正确性更好地识别保留/移除选择。

Conclusion: 答案正确性虽能提供信息，但不能决定轨迹价值。DHD能够测量这一缺失属性，并产生可复用的标签，用于学习智能体何时应"倾听"某个消息。研究揭示了错误消息中蕴含的推理价值，为多智能体系统的消息过滤策略提供了新的评估视角。

Abstract: Multi-agent reasoning systems often use agreement, confidence, or automated scores to decide which messages should shape a final answer. Such filtering assumes that a message likely to be correct is also worth keeping. Yet a wrong answer can contain a useful decomposition, constraint, or scientific principle. We test this distinction with Diverse Hypothesis Deliberation (DHD), a controlled measurement protocol that caches five independently generated messages and replays the same downstream solver, called the integrator, with each message available or hidden. The replay comparison measures a message's trajectory value: whether making the message available helps or harms subsequent reasoning. Across five mathematics and science benchmarks and two openly available model families, gpt-oss-120b and gemma-4-31B-it, wrong-helpful messages appear in every benchmark-model combination. Among wrong-answer messages that change final correctness, more than four in ten changes are helpful in each model. Controlled repeats show that the number of repeatable message effects is unlikely to arise from replay variation alone (p=0.0002). A focused intervention on repeatable wrong-helpful messages finds that the complete message works best, while retaining its reasoning preserves more success than retaining only its answer; the source of the complete-message advantage remains open. Within the same problem, repeated trajectory-value evidence also identifies a better keep-or-remove choice than answer correctness alone. Answer correctness is therefore informative but does not determine trajectory value. DHD measures this missing property and produces reusable labels for learning when agents should listen.

</details>


### [153] [Mandato: Protocol-Level Enforcement of Digitally Signed Mandates on AI Agent Actions with Cryptographically Chained Audit Trails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14074)
*Giovanni Racioppi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mandato，一个在协议层（MCP）强制实施数字签名授权令的治理代理。它为AI代理的工具调用引入加密签名的授权工件，提供可审计的决策日志，并映射到欧盟AI法案、GDPR、NIS2和eIDAS 2等法规框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理通过标准化工具调用协议（如MCP）与外部系统交互，但现有基础设施缺少对代理行为进行可验证授权的约束层。授权逻辑散落在应用代码中，既无签名也不可独立审计，导致日志缺乏证据价值，无法满足法律和合规要求。

Method: Mandato作为一个MCP透明的治理代理，在协议层面执行数字签名的授权令（mandate）。授权令是机器可读、加密签名的授权工件，指定代理可调用的工具、参数约束、上下文条件、有效期及委托方。代理对每个工具调用评估授权令链，在线拦截不合规调用，并记录所有决策（允许/拒绝及证据）于追加式哈希链审计日志中，通过限定时间戳定期锚定。架构上分离决策点与执行点，并参考民法中的授权委托制度设计授权令。文章还提供了该机制映射到欧盟AI法案第12/14条、GDPR问责、NIS2和eIDAS 2的合规路径，包括通过合格信任服务提供商（QTSP）实现合格认证的路线图。

Result: 给出了Mandato的授权令模型及其决策语义、参考架构（MCP透明代理，带分离的决策与执行点）、法规映射框架，以及参考系统的实现状态和定量评估计划（覆盖强制开销、审计完整性和篡改检测验证成本）。

Conclusion: Mandato填补了AI代理工具调用协议层缺乏可验证授权基础设施的空白，通过加密签名授权令和哈希链审计日志，将技术与法律合规（EU AI Act、GDPR、NIS2、eIDAS 2）对接，为代理行为提供可审计、有证据价值的治理机制。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act on external systems through standardized tool-calling protocols such as the Model Context Protocol (MCP), yet no infrastructure layer constrains their actions to what a principal has verifiably authorized: authorization logic lives in application code, is neither signed nor independently auditable, and the resulting logs lack evidentiary value. We present Mandato, a governance proxy that enforces digitally signed mandates on agent actions at the protocol level. A mandate is a machine-readable, cryptographically signed authorization artifact specifying which tools an agent may invoke, under which parameter constraints and contextual conditions, for how long, and on whose behalf; the proxy evaluates every tool call against the applicable mandate chain, blocks non-conforming calls in line, and records every decision -- permit, deny, and the evidence for each -- in an append-only, hash-chained audit log designed for evidentiary use and periodically anchored via qualified timestamps. The mandate is deliberately modeled on the civil-law institution of delegation of authority, making the artifact legible to lawyers and auditors, not only to engineers. We give the mandate model and its decision semantics, the reference architecture as an MCP-transparent proxy with separated decision and enforcement points, and a mapping of the mechanism onto EU AI Act Articles 12 and 14, GDPR accountability, NIS2, and eIDAS 2, including a roadmap to qualified attestation through Qualified Trust Service Providers (QTSPs). We describe the implementation status of the reference system and a quantitative evaluation plan covering enforcement overhead, audit completeness, and tamper-evidence verification cost.

</details>


### [154] [LLMs Don't Pay for the Jump](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14397)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨大语言模型无法进行溯因推理（abduction）的根本原因，主张关键缺失不在于具身模拟，而在于"认识论错误与物理代价之间的耦合"——即以普朗克提出E=hν解决黑体辐射问题为例，说明真正的溯因需要一种机制，使认识论错误产生足够的物理代价以强制系统修订假设，而固定权重的Transformer无论规模大小都缺乏这种耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（Zahavy 2026）认为LLM无法完成爱因斯坦等效原理式的溯因跳跃（abductive "Jump"），并将其归因于缺乏具身模拟；但Zheng-Xin和Farmer对此提出质疑，认为具身性并非溯因的必要条件。本文试图寻找比具身模拟更深层的解释，回答"机器溯因究竟缺什么"这一核心问题。

Method: 本文以普朗克1900年提出能量量子化假设E=hν为案例进行分析：（1）证明该假设既不能由归纳也不能由演绎产生；（2）论证其采纳依赖于"认识论错误与物理代价"之间的耦合，即经典理论预测无穷能量与实际有限测量值之间的矛盾在物理层面不可接受；（3）通过"热力学耦合"（thermodynamic coupling）的形式化框架区分这一机制；（4）结合实证结果——输出熵在不同因果难度任务上几乎不变，而准确率从100%降至17%——说明固定权重Transformer推理缺乏此类耦合。

Result: 论证表明，固定权重的Transformer推理无论模型规模如何，都缺乏认识论错误与物理代价之间的耦合机制；实证数据（输出熵在因果难度上升时保持稳定，准确率从100%跌至17%）与这一理论预测一致，支持"机器溯因缺失的要素比具身性更深层"的结论。

Conclusion: 机器溯因的真正缺失要素可能在于：系统必须拥有某种物理机制，使认识论错误变得足够昂贵以强制进行假设修订。这一发现将问题从"是否需要具身性"推进到"系统如何将错误转化为修订驱动力"的更深层次，为可进行溯因推理的AI系统设计指明了方向。

Abstract: Zahavy [2026] argues that Large Language Models, despite their capabilities in induction and deduction, cannot perform the abductive "Jump" that produced Einstein's equivalence principle, and attributes this limitation to the absence of embodied simulation. Zheng-Xin [2026] and Farmer [2026] question whether embodiment is necessary for abduction, pointing to alternative routes to General Relativity and forms of abduction that require no sensorimotor grounding. Max Planck resolved the blackbody radiation problem in 1900. Planck's move to E = hν required no embodied simulation. It was motivated by a mathematical consequence of classical theory, an infinite predicted energy for a finite measured quantity, that could not be physically accepted. We show that neither induction nor deduction could have produced the postulate and argue that its adoption required a coupling between epistemic error and physical cost. We formalize this distinction through thermodynamic coupling and show that fixed-weight transformer inference lacks such coupling, regardless of model scale. This is consistent with empirical results showing that output entropy remains nearly unchanged across tasks with sharply increasing causal difficulty, even as accuracy falls from 100% to 17%. We therefore argue that the missing ingredient in machine abduction may lie deeper than embodiment: a system must have some physical mechanism through which epistemic error becomes costly enough to force revision.

</details>


### [155] [A Pathway to General-Purpose Scientific AI: Multimodal Comprehension of Scientific Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14075)
*Jennifer D'Souza,Fahad Ahmed,Cecilia Andrea Bustamante Andrade,Lina Frolova,Poorani Gnanasambandan,Dilshad Hussain,Muhammad Uzair Khan,Nkembeng Kevin Nkengfoa,Paul Praveen J.,Fabio Priante,Sjoerd Franciscus van der Werf,Thomas Frederik Jan van Roeden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是ICDAR 2026竞赛（ALD/E-ImageMiner基准）的配套视角论文，探讨如何利用该基准（1951张图、205篇出版物）推动科学图像理解的未来方向，提出"科学概念理解"作为长期目标，并展望多模态科学AI的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表承载关键实验证据，但数字图书馆和多模态AI系统难以检索和解释它们。现有基准虽提供了分类、表格提取、摘要和VQA等任务，但缺乏对未来科学图像挑战方向的系统性思考，需要前瞻性视角来引导后续研究。

Method: 基于ALD/E-ImageMiner基准和ICDAR 2026竞赛的数据与任务设计，从任务能力探测（从视觉/定量阅读到领域推理和证据佐证）、Bloom分类学指导的问题设计等维度进行分析，并提出"科学概念理解"作为长期基准目标，涵盖更广泛领域、跨文档综合、假设评估、来源追踪、不确定性、反事实推理和开放式多模态研究等方向。

Result: 论文提出了一套面向科学图像理解的前瞻性研究议程：将"从图像中理解科学概念"确立为长期基准目标，并明确了八大未来方向，包括跨域/跨图表类型扩展、上下文与跨文档综合、假设检验、溯源、不确定性与反事实推理等，连接ICDAR竞赛与更广泛的可操作科学视觉知识议程。

Conclusion: 该视角论文将ICDAR 2026竞赛定位为更广泛"机器可操作的科学视觉知识与可验证的多模态科学AI"议程的一部分，为未来科学图像理解基准的设计提供了概念框架和路线图。

Abstract: Scientific figures and tables encode essential experimental evidence, yet remain difficult for digital libraries and multimodal AI systems to retrieve and interpret. The ALD/E-ImageMiner benchmark and ICDAR 2026 Competition on Information Extraction from Atomic Layer Deposition/Etching Scientific Figures provide 1,951 figures from 205 publications, expert-annotated for classification, data table extraction, summarization, and visual question answering. In these companion proceedings, we present a forward-looking perspective on how the benchmark can guide future scientific-image challenges. We examine how its tasks probe capabilities from visual and quantitative reading to domain-grounded reasoning and evidential justification, and how Bloom-informed question design can support deeper scientific understanding. We propose "scientific conceptual understanding from images" as a long-term benchmark objective, with future directions including broader domains and figure types, contextual and cross-document synthesis, hypothesis evaluation, provenance, uncertainty, counterfactual grounding, and open-ended multimodal research. This perspective connects the ICDAR 2026 challenge to a broader agenda for machine-actionable scientific visual knowledge and verifiable multimodal scientific AI.

</details>


### [156] [Split the Labor: Separating Evidence Interpretation from Decision Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14509)
*Zhelun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将"阅读并解释源材料"与"汇总解释得出结论"两个操作解耦，引入四字段证据元组（假说、可靠性桶、理据、来源），并揭示了一种称为"计数尺度漂移"（count-scale drift）的失败模式：对未归一化权重求和做阈值处理等价于后验阈值处理，但其操作点会随来源数量滑动。跨可靠性模型时，投票规则与后验对实例排序不同且无法通过阈值调和。解决方案是汇集校准的对数似然比（算术修复而非架构修复），并在纵向语料库上两次实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多数系统在让语言模型基于多个来源得出结论时，简单地将所有来源拼接进同一个提示（prompt）。作者指出这混淆了两个需求不同的操作：解读单个来源需要模型容量与上下文感知，而汇总各解读得出结论则需要固定的算术、跨实例可比性以及"不返回结论"的选项。将二者分离后，核心设计问题变成了二者之间的接口。作者寻求一种统一的、可泛化的框架来解决这一接口问题。

Method: 作者提出一个四字段证据元组（hypothesis, reliability bucket, rationale, provenance）作为两个操作之间的固定接口。通过固定该元组，同时确定了"解读"和"汇总"两半的设计。作者揭示了"计数尺度漂移"失败模式：对未归一化权重求和作阈值处理，在形式上等价于后验阈值处理，但操作点随来源数量滑动，且滑动幅度随阅读器可靠性增长。当各来源可靠性不同时，投票规则与后验对实例排序不一致，任何阈值都无法调和。解决方案是将校准后的对数似然比进行汇集，这是一个纯粹的算术修复而非架构修复，可推广到评分求和分诊引擎、按阳性计数打分的诊断面板以及加性多信号检测器。作者在纵向词料库上以两种粒度实例化该原则：结果发生后（按阅读粒度，关注读数）和结果发生前（按学习容量粒度，使用小序列编码器配合易辅助目标，加上承载删失生存损失的树集成）。

Result: 在结果发生前（前瞻性）的实例化中，小序列编码器加树集成（承载删失生存损失）达到0.921的AUPRC，而手工构建的基线仅0.805。论文还区分了哪些成分可跨域迁移、哪些必须按域重新估计，并给出了五个可证伪该框架的预测、三个负面结果，以及哪些比较仍存在混淆。

Conclusion: 将"解读来源"与"汇总解读"分离，并以四字段证据元组作为固定接口，是处理多源证据推理的正确框架。计数尺度漂移是此类系统的一个普遍失败模式，修复手段是算术性的（汇集校准对数似然比）而非架构性的。该原则同样适用于语言模型之外的各类评分求和系统，并在前瞻性与回顾性两种实例化中均奏效，只是粒度不同。作者明确提出可证伪性预测与负面结果，表明该框架在一定程度上是可检验而非仅凭直觉的。

Abstract: Systems that ask a language model to reach a conclusion from many sources usually concatenate them into one prompt. This conflates two operations with different requirements. Interpreting a source rewards capacity and context. Combining interpretations rewards fixed arithmetic, comparability across instances, and the option to return nothing. Once separated, the design problem becomes the interface between them. We propose a four-field evidence tuple (hypothesis, reliability bucket, rationale, provenance) and show that fixing it determines both halves. The separation also reveals a failure mode in how such systems combine, which we call count-scale drift. Thresholding a sum of unnormalized weights is exactly posterior thresholding, but at an operating point that slides with the number of sources consulted. The slide grows with reader reliability. When source reliabilities differ, the vote rule and the posterior order instances differently, and no threshold reconciles them. Pooling calibrated log-likelihood ratios addresses both problems. The fix is arithmetic rather than architectural, and applies to a class of rules beyond language models: score-summing triage engines, diagnostic panels scored by counting positives, and additive multi-signal detectors. We then instantiate the principle twice on one longitudinal corpus, once after outcomes resolve and once before. The same partition helps in both, at different granularities: over reading in the first, over learning capacity in the second. There, a small sequence encoder on an easy auxiliary objective plus a tree ensemble carrying the censored survival loss reaches 0.921 AUPRC against 0.805 for a hand-crafted baseline. We separate what transfers from what must be re-estimated per domain, and state five predictions that would falsify the framework, three negative results, and which comparisons remain confounded.

</details>


### [157] [Retrieval Grounding Latent Reasoning for Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14107)
*Gang Zhou,Xiongxi Yu,Hu Tian,Yang Wei,Lu Pan,Ke Zeng,Shibiao Xu,Xiaolong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RGLT是一种用于密集检索的潜在推理框架，通过将中间潜在状态转换与检索改进显式连接，解决了现有推理增强嵌入模型中潜在推理轨迹走捷径的问题。它结合了过程监督的显式到隐式蒸馏和检索接地监督，利用分阶段CoT重建塑造中间潜在状态，并通过检索效应信贷优化潜在推理轨迹的增量检索收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理增强嵌入模型虽然将推理信息融入稠密表示，但其监督主要由最终检索目标主导，导致潜在推理轨迹可能学习捷径模式——虽然保持检索性能却未产生有意义的增量检索收益。需要一种方法显式地将中间潜在转换与检索改进相关联。

Method: RGLT通过隐藏空间中的非自回归推理实现：1) 利用静默标记构建指令条件化的潜在推理轨迹；2) 结合过程监督的显式到隐式蒸馏（分阶段CoT重建）和检索接地监督（检索效应信贷），用于塑造中间潜在状态并优化跨轨迹的增量检索增益。

Result: 在推理密集型检索基准上，RGLT持续优于强基线模型，同时保持高效的嵌入推理性能（不增加推理开销）。

Conclusion: RGLT通过将潜在推理轨迹的每一步与检索改进显式接地，有效避免了捷径推理模式，实现了既提升推理密集型检索性能又能维持高效嵌入推理的双重目标。

Abstract: Reasoning-intensive retrieval requires text representations to capture not only semantic similarity, but also the reasoning needed to determine relevance under a given retrieval instruction. Existing reasoning-enhanced embedding models improve retrieval by incorporating reasoning information into dense representations, yet their supervision is typically dominated by the final retrieval objective. As a result, latent reasoning trajectories may learn shortcut reasoning patterns that preserve retrieval performance without producing meaningful incremental retrieval gains. We propose Retrieval Grounding Latent Reasoning (RGLT), a latent reasoning framework for dense retrieval that explicitly connects intermediate latent transitions with retrieval improvements. RGLT performs non-autoregressive reasoning in hidden space through an instruction-conditioned latent reasoning trajectory constructed from silent tokens. It combines process-supervised explicit-to-implicit distillation with retrieval-grounded supervision, using stage-wise CoT reconstruction to shape intermediate latent states and retrieval-effect credit to optimize incremental retrieval gains across the latent reasoning trajectories. Experiments on reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmarks show that RGLT consistently outperforms strong baselines while preserving efficient embedding inference.

</details>


### [158] [A Graph-Based Reinforcement Learning Framework for Structured Drift Diagnosis and Recovery in Autonomous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14109)
*Ismail El Hamraoui,Sagar Jose,Nicolas Bureau,Robert Plana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图的即插即用恢复模块，用单个经过强化学习训练的小语言模型在恢复图的每个节点上专门执行漂移分类、操作检测、风险评估和最终决策等任务，以检测和恢复自主LLM智能体的运行时行为漂移。在AppWorld基准上的实验表明该方法能用小模型发出正确的恢复决策，并严格遵循输出格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM智能体在复杂真实工作流中部署时容易发生运行时行为漂移，即偏离原始任务导致外部系统产生不可逆副作用。现有方法仅在提示层面处理漂移，缺乏结构化的步骤级检测、风险评估和恢复决策机制；且主任务执行体通常是大型昂贵模型，无法针对每次部署重训练，因此需要即插即用的恢复模块。

Method: 构建一个外置于主智能体的恢复图框架，用强化学习训练单个小语言模型，使其在每个图节点上专业化：节点角色包括漂移分类、操作检测、风险评估和最终决策。训练通过规则化结构奖励与LLM-as-judge语义质量信号相结合，使模型同时在回答方式（schema和长度）和回答内容上被评估。模型学习产出适配节点角色的结构化XML推理。

Result: 在公共AppWorld基准上的实验表明，该方法能够利用关于疑似漂移起点的信息，使用小语言模型发出正确的恢复决策；训练后的小模型可靠地遵守规定的输出schema，并在每个节点角色对应的字段中产生语义适当的内容。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了一个经过强化学习专门训练的小语言模型可以充当高效的即插即用恢复模块，在步骤级检测漂移、评估风险并做出恢复决策，同时保持严格的结构化输出，为主智能体提供低成本、可部署的运行时保护。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents are increasingly deployed in complex real-world workflows, yet they remain vulnerable to runtime behavioral drift, a silent deviation from the original task that can lead to irreversible side effects on external systems. Existing approaches address drift at the prompt level but lack structured mechanisms for step-level detection, risk assessment, and recovery decision. Because the main task-executing agent is often a large and expensive model that cannot be re-trained on every deployment, this work targets a plug-and-play recovery module instead. It introduces a graph-based framework in which a single small language model is trained via reinforcement learning to specialize at each node of a recovery graph, external to the main agent. Each node has a precise role\,: drift classification, operation detection, risk evaluation, or final decision and the model learns to produce structured XML-formatted reasoning adapted to that role. Training combines rule-based structural rewards with an LLM-as-judge semantic-quality signal, so that the model is graded both on how it answers (schema and length) and on what it says. Experiments on the public AppWorld benchmark show that the method generally exploits information about the suspected drift onset to issue correct recovery decisions using a small language model. In addition, the trained small language model reliably respects the prescribed output schema and produces semantically appropriate content in each field according to its assigned node role.

</details>


### [159] [Reinforcement Learning-Based Production Scheduling in an Industry-Based Coating Scenario Using the Digital Model Playground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14122)
*Arne Kröger,Ralf Buschermöhle,Wilhelm Hasselbring,Henrik Wilbers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文展示了强化学习（RL）在工业启发的涂层工艺生产调度中的适用性。使用开源的数字模型游乐场（DMPG）离散事件仿真框架建模并训练两种标准算法（DQN和PPO），与常规调度规则进行对比，结果显示PPO性能最为稳健，实现了关键性能指标的均衡改善。主要贡献在于弥合学术研究与工业实践之间的差距，提供了可复用的开源框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂制造环境中的生产调度需要同时处理顺序依赖的换线时间、随机扰动和交期约束，而现有的强化学习方法大多在简化的基准流程上验证，缺乏工业相关性。作者希望验证RL调度在反映实际复杂性（如顺序依赖换线时间、设备故障、利用率波动）的场景中的可行性，以弥合学术研究与工业应用之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用开源的DMPG离散事件仿真框架建模工业启发的涂层工艺场景，该场景包含顺序依赖换线时间、设备故障和可变利用率等实际复杂性。使用两种标准RL算法——Deep Q-Networks (DQN) 和 Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) 训练调度代理，并将其性能与常规调度规则（dispatching rules）进行基准对比。

Result: RL-based调度在关键性能指标上实现了均衡的改进，其中PPO展现出最稳健的性能表现。研究结果表明RL调度在具有实际复杂性的工业场景中是可行的，且优于传统调度规则。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于通过在一个真实可共享的工业场景中验证RL调度的有效性，弥合了学术研究与工业实践之间的差距，同时提供了一个可复用的开源框架(DMPG)供未来研究使用，为RL调度方法的进一步工业落地奠定了基础。

Abstract: Production scheduling in complex manufacturing environments is challenging when sequence-dependent setup times, stochastic disturbances, and due-date constraints must be addressed simultaneously. While reinforcement learning (RL) methods have shown promising results in research, most studies rely on simplified benchmark processes, limiting their industrial relevance. This paper demonstrates the applicability of RL-based scheduling in an industry-inspired coating process that reflects practical complexities such as sequence-dependent setup times, machine breakdowns, and variable utilization. The open-source Digital Model Playground (DMPG), a discrete event simulation framework, is used to model the scenario and to train RL agents. Two standard algorithms, Deep Q-Networks and Proximal Policy Optimization, are benchmarked against conventional dispatching rules to illustrate feasibility and to provide a transparent testbed for further research. Results indicate that RL-based scheduling achieves balanced improvements across key performance indicators, with PPO delivering the most robust performance. The main contribution of this work is to bridge the gap between academic research and industrial practice by validating RL-based scheduling in a realistic, shareable scenario and by providing a reusable open-source framework for future studies.

</details>


### [160] [Traj-LeWM: Path-Aware World-Model Planning via Latent Trajectory Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14125)
*Xiaodi Huang,Ziyi Ding,Jingtian Wan,Yuchen Liu,Yuan Zhang,Xiao-Ping Zhang,Jiayu Chen,Zhang Zhang,Tao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Traj-LeWM，一种轻量级视觉世界模型的改进版本，通过引入目标条件潜在轨迹代价（LTC）补充端点距离评分，在训练和规划两个阶段融合轨迹级信息，在Push-T、OGBench-Cube、Reacher和Two-Room四个基准上分别提升3、14、7、7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWM作为轻量级视觉世界模型存在两个局限：训练时仅学习局部下一步转移而未评估完整轨迹相对于任务目标的优劣；规划时仅依据预测端点距离排序候选动作，由于模型预测与实际执行存在偏差，端点最近的候选在真实环境中不一定表现最佳。完整预测轨迹的演化信息可以补充端点距离的不足。

Method: Traj-LeWM保留LeWM的局部动力学目标与端点评分，同时引入目标条件潜在轨迹代价（LTC）作为补充信号。训练阶段，基于LTC的轨迹偏好监督与下一步预测共同塑造共享表征；规划阶段，LTC与端点距离联合用于候选动作排序，融入中间路径信息。

Result: Traj-LeWM在Push-T、OGBench-Cube、Reacher和Two-Room四个任务上分别比LeWM提升3、14、7、7个百分点。受控实验与消融研究验证了轨迹级表征塑造与路径感知候选排序的互补作用。

Conclusion: 轨迹级信息的引入能够在训练和规划两个阶段有效补充端点级信号，从而提升视觉世界模型在目标导向任务上的规划性能。

Abstract: LeWM is a lightweight visual world model that learns latent dynamics end-to-end from pixels and ranks candidate action sequences by the distance between their predicted endpoints and the goal. However, LeWM has two limitations. First, during training, it learns local next-step transitions without evaluating complete trajectories relative to the task goal. Second, during planning, it ranks candidates solely by predicted endpoint distance. Because model predictions may differ from actual execution outcomes, the candidate whose predicted endpoint is closest to the goal may not perform best when executed in the environment. The evolution of the complete predicted trajectory can therefore provide complementary information beyond endpoint distance. To address these limitations, we propose Traj-LeWM, which retains LeWM's local-dynamics objective and endpoint score while introducing a goal-conditioned latent trajectory cost (LTC) that aggregates trajectory-level information as a complementary signal. During training, LTC-based trajectory-preference supervision complements next-step prediction in shaping the shared representation. During planning, LTC is combined with endpoint distance to incorporate intermediate-path information into candidate ranking. With joint endpoint-plus-LTC scoring, Traj-LeWM outperforms LeWM on Push-T, OGBench-Cube, Reacher, and Two-Room by $3$, $14$, $7$, and $7$ percentage points, respectively. Controlled experiments and ablations further verify the complementary roles of trajectory-level representation shaping and path-aware candidate ranking.

</details>


### [161] [QuaSAR: Quantization Compensation via Stable Activation-Aware Rank Truncation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14149)
*Lin-Fa Lee,Yi-Yu Chang,Kuo-Hei Yeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有训练后量化（PTQ）方法中基于拟合优度的门控机制在低比特W4A4设置下会将数值求解器不稳定导致的负拟合分数误判为不可补偿层并丢弃，从而损失精度恢复潜力。作者提出一种无参数的截断伪逆求解器，在求逆前移除退化方向，解决秩亏输入激活导致的病态Gram矩阵问题。在ViT-B上W4A4设置下达到81.42%的top-1准确率，联合低秩与量化压缩后可在54.7 MB模型下实现80.26%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练后量化方法通过闭式残差补偿恢复精度，但由于门控机制无法区分"预测差的量化误差"与"数值求解器失败"两种情况：秩亏输入激活会导致Gram矩阵严重病态或数值奇异，使闭式求解器不稳定并产生虚假的负拟合分数，导致本可恢复精度提升的层被误丢弃。

Method: 提出参数自由的截断伪逆（truncated pseudoinverse）求解器，在矩阵求逆前先移除塌缩方向（collapsed directions），从而规避由于秩亏输入激活导致的病态或奇异Gram矩阵问题，实现数值稳定的闭式残差补偿求解。

Result: 在ViT-B上W4A4设置下达到81.42%的top-1准确率，超越先前后训练方法及基于微调的基线；联合低秩与量化压缩后达到80.26%准确率、54.7 MB模型大小的部署点，在模型体积与精度之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: 现有基于拟合优度的门控机制在低比特量化下存在数值求解器失败导致的误判问题，通过数值稳定求解器可在不增加参数开销的前提下恢复更多被错误丢弃层的误差补偿能力，从而在无需训练的情况下进一步提升量化模型精度。

Abstract: Recent training-free post-training quantization methods restore model accuracy through closed-form residual compensation. To constrain additional model storage overhead, several existing methods gate layer selection by goodness-of-fit, retaining only those layers whose compensation yields a positive residual fit score and discarding the rest. In this paper, we show that, under the low-bit W4A4 setting, this gating mechanism fails to distinguish poorly predictable quantization error from numerical solver failure. Rank-deficient input activations yield severely ill-conditioned or numerically singular Gram matrices, causing the closed-form solver to become unstable and produce spuriously negative fit scores. Consequently, existing goodness-of-fit gates misclassify affected layers as uncompensable and discard them. Many of these discarded layers can nevertheless provide substantial error recovery when their compensation is computed using a numerically stable solver. To address this problem, we propose a parameter-free truncated pseudoinverse solver which removes collapsed directions prior to inversion. On ViT-B with the W4A4 setting, our training-free method achieves 81.42\% top-1 accuracy, outperforming prior post-training methods and fine-tuning-based baselines. Combined with joint low-rank and quantization compression, the proposed method reaches a deployable operating point of 80.26\% accuracy at 54.7 MB, providing a well-balanced trade-off between model size and accuracy.

</details>


### [162] [Towards Efficient Multimodal and Multilingual Opinion Extraction for STI: A QLoRA-Based Fine-Tuning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14152)
*Sheng Hong,Xuanqi Wang,Jiacheng Wang,Yuwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向科技情报（STI）的多模态核心观点提取框架，利用视觉证据作为文本判断的上下文锚点，通过VideoLLaMA2（VL2/VL2.1）结合QLoRA微调，在2,194个多语言多模态样本上实现结构化JSON输出，并在后处理阶段引入基于模糊累积前景理论的分诊模块进行案例级价值评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽推动了语义分析发展，但在科技情报领域的观点提取任务中，现成模型难以从海量信息流中过滤噪声，且在零样本多语言、多模态场景下结构化输出可靠性有限，存在信息过载和提取聚焦不足的问题。

Method: 提出多模态核心观点提取框架：以视觉证据作为文本判断的上下文锚点；基于VideoLLaMA2和VideoLLaMA2.1基础模型，在2,194个多语言多模态样本上应用QLoRA低秩自适应微调；后处理阶段集成基于模糊累积前景理论（Fuzzy Cumulative Prospect Theory）的分诊模块，进行案例级价值评估。

Result: 在Image-Augmented设置下，微调后的VL2.1生成结构化JSON核心观点输出，达到64.98%精确率、42.15%召回率、51.14% F1分数和74.00%样本级准确率；相比零样本VL2.1，西班牙语和俄语的F1分数分别从4.83%和0.45%大幅提升至46.05%和51.93%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过视觉锚定和微调有效缓解了信息过载和提取聚焦问题，显著提升了低资源语言的零样本性能，并通过模糊累积前景理论分诊模块为下游科技情报筛选提供案例级价值信号。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have reshaped semantic analysis. Opinion Extraction (OE) for Science and Technology Intelligence (STI) requires concise core opinions from large information streams. Off-the-shelf models struggle to filter noise from these streams and show limited structured-output reliability in zero-shot multilingual and multi-modal settings. To address information overload and extraction defocus, this study proposes a multimodal core-opinion extraction framework in which visual evidence serves as a contextual anchor for textual judgment. Using VideoLLaMA2 (VL2) and VideoLLaMA2.1 (VL2.1) as the base models, we apply Quantized Low-Rank Adaptation (QLoRA) fine-tuning on a curated dataset of 2,194 multilingual and multimodal samples. Under the selected Image-Augmented setting, fine-tuned VL2.1 generates structured JSON core-opinion outputs, achieving 64.98% Precision, 42.15% Recall, 51.14% F1-score, and 74.00% sample-level accuracy. Relative to the zero-shot VL2.1 setting, it raises the F1-scores of Spanish and Russian from 4.83% and 0.45% to 46.05% and 51.93%, respectively. The framework further incorporates a Fuzzy Cumulative Prospect Theory-based post-extraction triage module for case-level value assessment, providing a case-level value signal for downstream STI screening.

</details>


### [163] [Removing Temporal Note Redundancy Improves Multimodal Reinforcement Learning for Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14157)
*Chenran Weng,Joo Seung Lee,Malini Mahendra,Anil Aswani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种冗余感知的多模态状态表示框架，在强化学习策略学习前显式去除临床笔记中的时间冗余文本（如复制前文、模板化文字），以提升机械通气决策支持中的状态表示质量和RL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械通气需要动态调整呼吸机设置，强化学习是优化这类序贯决策的有力框架，但标准方法仅依赖结构化EHR数据，忽略了自由文本临床笔记中的关键信息。然而，临床笔记中大量时间冗余（复制前文、模板化、重复记录）会稀释局部时间更新，降低状态表示质量，因此需要解决将纵向临床笔记有效整合到RL状态空间的问题。

Method: 提出冗余感知的多模态状态表示框架，在策略学习前显式去除随时间的重复笔记文本。评估两种高效的时间分解策略：（1）基于嵌入空间的分解，利用局部历史子空间上的奇异值分解（SVD）去除重复文本；（2）可解释的句子级diff操作，在文本编码前过滤掉先前已记录的句子。均用于构建融合结构化数据与去冗余文本的RL状态表示。

Result: 在真实ICU数据上，通过剥离时间笔记冗余构建的状态表示在四种离策略评估方法（Model-Based Rollouts、Fitted Q-Evaluation、Weighted Importance Sampling、Weighted Doubly Robust Evaluation）下均显著优于仅结构化数据和无冗余处理的原始笔记基线。

Conclusion: 显式地从重复笔记文本中分离新的临床信息能够产生更高质量的状态表示，直接提升强化学习在临床决策支持中的性能。

Abstract: Mechanical ventilation is a critical life-support intervention, requiring dynamic adjustments to ventilator settings as a patient's condition evolves. While reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising framework for optimizing these sequential decisions, standard approaches rely primarily on structured electronic health record (EHR) data, missing crucial clinical context recorded in free-text notes. Integrating longitudinal clinical notes into RL state spaces is challenging because notes are heavily inflated by temporal redundancy, such as copy-forward text, templating, and repetitive documentation, which dilutes time-local updates and degrades state representation quality. To address this, we propose a redundancy-aware multimodal state representation framework that explicitly removes duplicated note text over time before policy learning. We evaluate two computationally efficient temporal decomposition strategies for removing duplicated note text: (1) an embedding-space decomposition using singular value decomposition on local history subspaces, and (2) an interpretable sentence-level diff operation that filters out previously documented sentences before text encoding. Using real-world ICU data, we demonstrate that state representations constructed by stripping temporal note redundancy significantly outperform both structured-only and raw-note baselines across multiple off-policy evaluation methods (Model-Based Rollouts, Fitted Q-Evaluation, Weighted Importance Sampling, and Weighted Doubly Robust Evaluation). Our findings show that explicitly isolating new clinical information from repeated note text yields higher-quality state representations and directly improves RL performance for clinical decision support.

</details>


### [164] [BiasTrace: Linking Reasoning Behaviours to Biased Outputs in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14161)
*Varsha Ramineni,Hossein A. Rahmani,Jerome Ramos,Karin Sevegnani,Emine Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BiasTrace，一个用于标注LLM推理轨迹中偏倚相关行为的注释方案，将推理行为与偏倚输出联系起来。研究发现偏倚输出往往源于微妙的推理行为而非显式偏倚语言，推理级注释能提升偏倚检测性能，并可应用于推理时缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM偏倚研究主要关注最终输出，对产生偏倚结果的内部机制理解有限。虽然LLM推理能力的新进展为研究偏倚提供了新视角，但推理与偏倚之间的联系仍不明确，已有方法仅关注最终答案正确性或显式偏倚语言，忽略了推理中驱动偏倚结果的不同行为。

Method: 提出BiasTrace注释方案，用于标注模型生成推理轨迹中的推理行为并将其与偏倚结果关联。该方案捕获偏倚特定行为（如不支持的群体假设）以及可能隐式促成偏倚的通用推理模式（如过度思考）。在偏倚敏感场景中应用BiasTrace，使用经过验证的LLM-as-a-judge方法进行规模化注释，构建大规模标注数据集。

Result: 分析表明偏倚输出往往源于微妙的推理行为而非显式偏倚语言；推理级注释提升了偏倚检测性能；BiasTrace所捕获的行为可被利用进行推理时偏倚缓解。

Conclusion: 研究强调了检查更广泛推理模式对于更好理解LLM偏倚的重要性，为偏倚检测和缓解提供了推理层面的新途径。

Abstract: LLMs exhibit social biases that can produce inaccurate and discriminatory inferences, posing risks in high-stakes applications. While prior work has made progress in measuring and mitigating bias, it largely focuses on final outputs of models, with limited understanding of the mechanisms that produce biased outcomes. Recent advances in LLM reasoning offers a new lens for investigating bias, yet the link between reasoning and bias remains poorly understood. Existing approaches focus primarily on final answer correctness or explicitly biased language, overlooking different behaviours in reasoning that can drive biased outcomes. We introduce BiasTrace, an annotation scheme for labelling reasoning behaviours in model-generated traces and linking them to biased outcomes. BiasTrace captures bias-specific behaviours (e.g., unsupported demographic assumptions) as well as general reasoning patterns that may implicitly contribute to bias (e.g. overthinking). We apply BiasTrace to reasoning traces in bias-sensitive contexts, scaled using validated LLM-as-a-judge methods, producing a large annotated dataset. Our analysis shows that biased outputs often stem from subtle reasoning behaviours rather than explicitly biased language, and that reasoning-level annotations improve bias detection. We further show that BiasTrace behaviours can be exploited for inference-time mitigation. These findings underscore the importance of examining a broader range of reasoning patterns to better understand bias in LLMs.

</details>


### [165] [Can Language Models Understand mmWave Data? Benchmarking Large Language Models for mmWave Radar-Based Human Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14179)
*Jeongwan Shin,Jaehyeon Kim,Donguk Ko,Jaeho Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出mmWave-QA，第一个用于语言条件毫米波人体感知的基准，通过最简文本化接口将毫米波点云序列化为自然语言，使现成的LLM无需重新训练即可在QA设置下执行雷达感知推理，并展示了零样本推理能力及在视觉退化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM具有强大的推理和生成能力，但将其与毫米波模态集成的研究几乎空白。主要瓶颈包括雷达-语言配对数据稀缺、跨数据集异质性严重以及缺乏基础毫米波编码器。尽管VLMs已尝试将推理能力融入视觉感知，毫米波在低光和遮挡条件下的独特优势尚未被LLM充分利用。

Method: 提出一个最小化文本化接口，将每个毫米波点云序列化为简洁的自然语言，使现成的LLM能在问答（QA）设置下直接运行。在此基础上构建mmWave-QA基准，聚合异构的公开毫米波数据集，通过校准感知预处理和全局分类体系对齐进行规范化处理，并提供自然语言QA标注。基准覆盖六个场景和五个QA任务。

Result: mmWave-QA提供了跨不同毫米波硬件和实验条件的标准化评估平台。在基准上评估和分析多种LLM，展示了其在雷达感知上的零样本推理潜力，以及在视觉退化条件下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: mmWave-QA建立了可扩展的毫米波-LLM集成研究基础，验证了LLM无需专门训练即可通过自然语言接口理解毫米波点云数据的方法可行性，为后续研究提供了标准化评测平台和方法论参考。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable reasoning and generative capabilities, motivating their use as universal reasoning engines for perception. While modern approaches such as vision-language models (VLMs) have attempted to incorporate reasoning capabilities into visual sensing, the integration of LLMs with the millimeter-wave (mmWave) modality-despite its unique advantages under low light and occlusion-remains largely unexplored. The principal bottlenecks stem from the scarcity of radar language pairs, severe cross-dataset heterogeneity, and the absence of a foundational mmWave encoder. We address this gap through a minimal textualization interface that serializes each mmWave point cloud into concise natural language, allowing off-the-shelf LLMs to operate in a question answering (QA) setting. Building on this, we present mmWave-QA, the first benchmark for language-conditioned mmWave human perception. mmWave-QA aggregates heterogeneous public mmWave datasets and harmonizes them via calibration-aware preprocessing and global taxonomy alignment, while providing natural language QA. Spanning six scenarios and five QA tasks, the benchmark enables standardized evaluation across diverse mmWave hardware and experimental conditions, establishing a foundation for scalable research on mmWave-LLM integration. We further evaluate and analyze LLMs on our mmWave-QA, highlighting their zero-shot reasoning potential for radar perception, as well as their robustness under visual degradation.

</details>


### [166] [FreeBalance: Pre-Routing Online Moe Load Balancing via Residual Workload Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14205)
*Pengfei Chen,Yize Wu,Shouxu Kuang,Ke Gao,Ling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FreeBalance是一个无损的在线负载均衡框架，通过残差工作负载预测将专家迁移与前序计算阶段（如注意力）重叠，从而在不增加推理关键路径开销的情况下解决MoE模型专家并行中的负载不均衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型专家并行推理中，负载不均衡导致的rank过载会阻塞全局执行并增加延迟。现有在线负载均衡方法依赖路由决策后的统计数据，使得专家权重加载/迁移必须在路由确定后才能开始，迁移开销落在推理关键路径上。因此，需要一种能提前预测路由分布、将迁移与计算重叠的方案。

Method: FreeBalance利用残差网络中隐藏表示的跨层相似性构建轻量级工作负载预测器，在路由决策可用之前预测路由分布，从而实现专家迁移的前瞻性规划。通过将权重迁移与计算密集的前路由阶段（如注意力）重叠来隐藏同步开销；同时引入成本模型约束交换次数，确保迁移完全隐藏于可用时间窗口内。

Result: 实验表明，FreeBalance将最大-平均rank负载比降低了32.8%，端到端prefill延迟降低了13.1%。每层平均隐藏了5.1个专家的均衡开销，否则这些开销将占据关键路径延迟的约8.5%。

Conclusion: 通过预测驱动的前瞻性迁移重叠策略，FreeBalance在保持无损失（lossless）的前提下有效解决了在线负载均衡的开销问题，显著提升了MoE分布式推理的效率。

Abstract: Load imbalance poses a major bottleneck to the efficiency of expert parallelism in distributed inference of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. The most heavily loaded rank stalls global execution due to skewed routing distributions, directly increasing latency. While offline expert placement can alleviate persistent imbalance, practical multi-task serving workloads exhibit layer- and batch-dependent routing dynamics, making online load balancing indispensable. Existing approaches rely on routing statistics collected after each MoE router, requiring expert weight load or migration to begin only after routing decisions are available, consequently placing migration overhead on the inference critical path. In this work, we observe that online balancing can instead be largely overlapped with computation before target routing (e.g., attention), if routing distributions can be predicted accurately in advance. Therefore, we propose FreeBalance, a lossless online load-balancing framework that overlaps expert migration with preceding computation stages via residual workload prediction. FreeBalance leverages cross-layer similarities in hidden representations within the residual network to build a lightweight workload predictor. This enables proactive expert migration planning before routing decisions are available, creating substantial overlap between weight transfer and computation-heavy pre-routing stages. Furthermore, a cost model constrains the number of swaps to fully hide the synchronization overhead within the available window. Experiments across models and datasets show that FreeBalance reduces the max-to-mean rank load ratio by 32.8% and end-to-end prefill latency by 13.1%. Specifically, our method hides balancing overhead of an average of 5.1 experts per layer, which would otherwise account for about 8.5% of the critical-path latency.

</details>


### [167] [APTER: Adaptive Post-Training with Expert-Grounded Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14212)
*Xukai Wang,Liangqi Li,Zhiyue Xu,Jingang Zhou,Xiaoyu Shi,Jiansheng Cai,Bo Zhang,Zhe Li,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出APTER框架，将专家构建的结构化领域知识融入大语言模型的细粒度评估、优化与诊断，通过专家准则构建查询级评分标准，并利用评分裁决同时驱动优化和标准级能力缺陷诊断，在数学推理和医学问答任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型进入专业领域时需满足领域约束、包含关键证据、提供完整推理，而现有后训练方法依赖整体偏好或结果级验证，基于rubric的方法又常为每个查询独立生成rubric，导致在专业领域可能遗漏关键要求且跨样本不稳定，难以诊断和修复持续性的能力缺陷。

Method: APTER包含两个核心模块：(1) 专家标准的rubric构建——从领域专家构建的标准框架出发，每个标准代表稳定的专业能力；对每个查询选取相关标准并实例化为链接到源标准的查询级rubric，将可复用的专家标准转化为无需参考答案的可执行查询级监督。(2) 自适应后训练——将rubric裁决同时作为优化信号和标准级诊断信号，按标准ID聚合低分裁决以揭示持续缺陷，并在强化学习训练中触发针对性监督微调更新。

Result: 在数学推理和医学问答两个领域均取得一致的性能提升。跨三代模型，APTER相对于对应基础模型在数学和医学平均分上分别最高提升15.86和8.04分。代码和rubric数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 将专家领域知识以结构化标准的形式融入后训练的细粒度评估与诊断，能够有效识别并针对性修复模型在专业复杂推理中的持续性能力缺陷，相比基于整体偏好或独立生成rubric的方法在专业领域表现更好。

Abstract: As large language models enter professional domains, they must satisfy domain constraints, include critical evidence, and provide complete reasoning rather than merely produce fluent responses. Existing post-training methods often rely on holistic preferences or outcome-level verification, while recent rubric-based methods usually generate rubrics independently for each query. In specialized domains, such unconstrained rubrics may omit critical requirements and vary across samples, hindering the diagnosis and targeted repair of persistent capability deficiencies. We propose APTER (Adaptive Post-Training with Expert-Grounded Rubrics), a framework that integrates structured domain knowledge into fine-grained evaluation, optimization, and diagnosis for specialized complex reasoning. First, expert-grounded rubric construction starts from an expert criteria framework built by domain experts, where each criterion represents a stable professional capability. For each query, APTER selects relevant criteria and instantiates them into query-level rubrics linked to their source criteria, turning reusable expert criteria into executable query-level supervision without reference answers. Second, adaptive post-training uses rubric verdicts as both optimization and criterion-level diagnostic signals. Aggregating low-scoring verdicts by criterion ID reveals persistent deficiencies and triggers targeted supervised fine-tuning updates during reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and medical question answering show consistent gains across both domains. Across three model generations, APTER improves the mathematics and medical averages over the corresponding base models by up to 15.86 and 8.04 points, respectively. Code and rubric datasets are available at https://github.com/AntDT-APTER/APTER.

</details>


### [168] [A Generalized Parallelogram Rule for Proportional Analogies on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14220)
*Pierre-Alexandre Murena,Marcelo Hartmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在黎曼流形上引入比例类比关系，将欧几里得空间中用于算术类比的平行四边形规则推广到非欧几里得空间，并在球面、形状空间和概率分布流形等流形上进行了示例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比例类比在符号域和向量空间中已有广泛研究，但在非欧几里得空间中的使用受到限制。现有的类比框架无法直接应用于黎曼流形这类弯曲空间，因此需要将比例类比的概念扩展到黎曼域中。

Method: 作者在黎曼流形上定义了比例类比关系，将欧几里得空间中的平行四边形规则推广到黎曼几何框架下，利用流形的测地线结构来构造类比关系 a : b :: c : d。

Result: 在多种流形上展示了所提出的类比关系，包括球面、形状空间以及概率分布流形，验证了该定义的适用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将比例类比系统地引入黎曼域，为在弯曲空间中建立类比推理提供了理论基础，可望推动类比在几何数据分析中的进一步应用。

Abstract: Analogies are quaternary relations of the form "a is to b as c is to d", usually denoted a : b :: c : d. This notion is formalized in particular with the notion of proportional analogy, which imposes some constraints on the valid analogies. Whereas proportional analogies have been studied mostly in symbolic domains and in vector spaces, their use is limited in non-Euclidean spaces. In this paper, we introduce a proportional analogy relation in Riemannian domains, extending the parallelogram rule used for arithmetic analogies in Euclidean spaces. We illustrate the introduced analogy on various manifolds, such as the sphere, shape spaces and manifolds of probability distributions.

</details>


### [169] [Attributing Preprocessing Invariance in Spectral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14227)
*Dongjun Wei,Hongyi Wu,Yinuo Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑了"预处理不变性"作为光谱基础模型学习证据的解读。以拉曼基础模型为例，作者发现模型内置的归一化层（无学习参数）本身就足以消除某些预处理差异（即一个光谱等于另一个的正倍数加常数），模型并未在此类不变性上超过其自身归一化层。在六个拉曼数据集上，模型的表现未显著优于其归一化层；训练虽改善了编码器本身，但仅当变换到达编码器时它才会学习忽略该变换。跨五种模态的多个系统中，大多数归一化已移除此类变换，却仍宣称其为学习所得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光谱基础模型常以预处理不变性作为其学习能力的证据，但作者指出这种推理存在缺陷：模型在应用任何学习参数之前就对输入进行归一化，如果归一化本身就能将不同方式预处理的谱图映射到同一向量，则此类不变性不能归因于学习。作者希望通过系统性实验重新审视这一结论，澄清"不变性"究竟来自学习还是预处理的归一化。

Method: 作者以拉曼基础模型为案例，分析其归一化（基于每个谱图自身统计量）的性质，指出当且仅当两个谱图互为"正倍数加常数"时它们会被映射到同一向量。作者在六个拉曼评估数据集上比较模型输出与其归一化层单独输出的性能；通过受控实验检验训练是否仅在变换到达编码器时才学会忽略该变换；用数值测试判断给定归一化移除哪些变换；并在多种模态的发布系统中复现比较。
